## 📄 Page 001 (P001): A - M FIRST WORDS
- **Section:** A-Z Alphabet
- **Canonical Object:** `alphabet_a_to_m`
- **Judge Verdict:** `APPROVED` (Score: `97.5/100` | Winner: `AGT-002-DESIGN`)
- **Judge Rationale:** Synthesized hybrid specification for A - M FIRST WORDS. Locked bold outline style with zero shading, enforced vertical portrait 3:4 framing, preserved 0.50in margin clearance, enforced container uniformity, and enforced pure inanimate object (no facial features) rule.

### 💬 Round 1: Specialist Agent Proposals
| Specialist Agent | Perspective & Proposals | Constraints / Prohibitions |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **AGT-002-DESIGN** | **composition:** Balanced educational flashcard spread for A - M FIRST WORDS. 100% equal-sized uniform rounded-corner cards across all rows, vertically centered content with balanced margins.<br/>**line_weight:** Thick 6pt bold black vector outlines enclosing large, open coloring shapes. | empty grid boxes<br/>blank cells<br/>intersecting table grid<br/>widescreen 16:9 crop<br/>unequal box heights<br/>empty top voids |
| **AGT-003-KDP** | **geometry:** Strict 0.50in (150px) margin safety clearance from canvas boundary, 2550x3300px at 300 DPI, zero interior bleed.<br/>**compliance:** Pure binary black & white (#000000 / #FFFFFF). Zero grayscale or drop-shadows. |  |
| **AGT-004-MARKET** | **commercial_appeal:** High parent perceived value for early childhood literacy. All living animals/characters MUST feature sweet smiling faces, while inanimate objects must remain clean and faceless. Exact 1-to-1 counting accuracy. | faceless animals<br/>faces on fruits/objects<br/>scary silhouettes<br/>counting mismatches |
| **AGT-005-EDU** | **pedagogical_hook:** Direct letter/numeral-to-illustration correspondence. Card assignments sourced from manifest/pages.json cards array.<br/>**target_milestone:** Ages 1-4 early phonetic awareness and numeracy. |  |

### 🔍 Round 3: Adversarial Red-Team Stress-Test (AGT-006)
**Stress-Test Findings:**
- Ensure AI renders 100% equal-sized containers without tall bottom boxes.
- Ensure AI centers content vertically inside cards without empty top voids.
- Ensure AI renders exact object counts per card as specified in manifest cards array.
- Ensure AI does not draw cartoon eyes on inanimate food/objects.
- Ensure all numerals rendered as HOLLOW BUBBLE OUTLINES with white interior, not solid black.
**Recommended Hardening:** Merge negative tokens from curriculum.yaml spread_style.common_negative_tokens + layout_templates.shared_negative_tokens + per-card negative_tokens.

### ⚖️ Round 4: Judge Decision & Locked Prompts
- **Positive Prompt (Copy & Paste):**
  ```text
  Educational preschool toddler alphabet coloring poster. 13 identical uniform rounded-corner square flashcard boxes of equal dimensions arranged in 4 centered balanced rows (Row 1: 4 cards; Row 2: 4 cards; Row 3: 3 cards centered; Row 4: 2 cards centered). NUMERAL STYLE MANDATE: Every numeral and letter in every card MUST be a LARGE BOLD HOLLOW BUBBLE OUTLINE with PURE WHITE INTERIOR FILL and thick black 5pt closed contour stroke — bubbly rounded preschool font style, white inside the numeral/letter, strictly NOT solid black filled, NOT dark filled. CONTAINER UNIFORMITY: All flashcard containers have 100% identical width and height. Strictly prohibit unequal box heights, tall bottom boxes, or distorted last-row cards. VERTICAL CENTERING: Inside each card, the hollow bubble numeral/letter on the left and the line art on the right are vertically centered at Y=50% of the card, filling 75% of card height with equal balanced top and bottom padding. Strictly NO empty top voids, NO content sagging to bottom. Inside each rounded card, the large bold HOLLOW BUBBLE uppercase letter on the left and the simple preschool illustration on the right are vertically centered with balanced top and bottom padding: A with pure physical Apple; B with clean Soccer Ball; C with cute friendly Cat with smiling face and round eyes; D with cute friendly Puppy Dog with smiling face; E with cute friendly baby Elephant with smiling face; F with cute friendly Fish with smiling eye; G with clean bunch of Grapes; H with clean physical Hat; I with clean Ice Cream Cone; J with clean Jam Jar; K with clean flying Kite; L with cute friendly Lion with smiling face; M with clean Crescent Moon and stars. STRICT TAXONOMY: ONLY living animals and characters have friendly facial features. ALL inanimate objects are pure physical inanimate objects with strictly NO eyes, NO mouth, NO face, NO facial features, non-anthropomorphic. Thick bold black vector outlines, 6pt stroke, wide white spacing between individual cards, perfectly centered on vertical portrait 3:4 canvas, pure stark white background (#FFFFFF), zero shading, zero grayscale, zero gradients, strictly NO full-page intersecting grid lines, NO connected table borders, NO empty blank boxes.
  ```
- **Negative Prompt:**
